    "tarball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/tarball/v2026.5.11",
    "zipball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/zipball/v2026.5.11",
    "body": "## Added\n- **(security)** Verify and record provenance during `mise lock`, with a new `provenance_api_failures_fatal` setting to control whether GitHub attestation API failures are fatal ([#9945](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9945) by @jdx).\n- **(security)** Fall back to verifying archive contents when SLSA provenance attests every file inside an archive but not the archive itself, fixing releases like `github:prefix-dev/pixi@0.68.1` ([#9898](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9898) by @sargunv).\n- **(plugins)** Support remote git subdirectory sources for plugins, e.g. `git::https://host/repo.git//path/to/plugin?ref=branch` ([#9893](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9893) by @jdx).\n\n## Fixed\n- **(github)** Asset picker now picks the shortest matching name as a tiebreaker for `asset_pattern` and accepts platform-agnostic runtime archives like `.phar`, `.jar`, and `.pyz` (fixes installing `composer`) ([#9946](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9946) by @jdx).\n- **(config)** Invalid `miserc.toml` now produces a clear parse error at startup instead of being silently ignored ([#9937](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9937) by @jdx).\n- **(install)** Per-tool `.mise.backend.toml` metadata is now written alongside install directories, making merged/copied installs self-describing and refreshing install state mid-run so same-run dependency resolution sees freshly installed tools ([#9941](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9941) by @jdx).\n- **(install)** `postinstall` hooks now run through the configured default inline shell instead of `$SHELL -c` ([#9812](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9812) by @risu729).\n- **(cache)** `mise cache prune [PLUGIN]...` now honors the plugin filter instead of pruning every cache directory ([#9914](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9914) by @risu729).\n- **(task)** Preserve task-declared env, `MISE_TASK_*` metadata, and `MISE_ENV` across nested `hook-env` invocations, while keeping the nested-PATH fix from [#9765](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9765) intact ([#9850](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9850) by @risu729).\n- **(backend)** Resolve helper dependency toolsets in offline mode so `minimum_release_age` cannot mis-route helper tools like `node`/`npm` when querying upstream versions ([#9808](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9808) by @risu729).\n- **(vfox)** Key vfox `EnvKeys` hooks by the resolved install path so shared/system installs don't reuse user-path cache entries ([#9907](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9907) by @risu729).\n- **(use)** Skip the `mise use -g` shadow warning when the active version comes from system config ([#9900](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9900) by @risu729).\n- **(doctor)** List installed plugins from install state, including those owned by disabled backends, and add a `plugins` object to `mise doctor -J` ([#9863](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9863) by @risu729).\n- **(erlang)** `erlang.compile = false` is now strict precompiled mode and no longer falls back to `kerl build-install` on unsupported distros ([#9866](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9866) by @risu729).\n\n## Changed\n- **(registry)** Prefer the `aqua` backend for `cilium-hubble`, `localstack`, `mark`, `openbao`, `porter`, `process-compose`, `rtk`, `sqlc`, `turso`, and `xcodegen`, with existing GitHub/asdf backends preserved as fallbacks ([#9789](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9789) by @risu729).\n- **(registry)** Add `aqua:jbangdev/jbang` as the primary backend for `jbang`, enabling Windows support ([#9811](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9811) by @risu729).\n- **(registry)** Alias `dotnet-core` to `dotnet` ([#9807](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9807) by @risu729).\n- **(registry)** Add [`lisette`](https://lisette.run/) ([#9944](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9944) by @ivov).\n- **(registry)** Fix `sourcery` archive format so macOS installs use the `.zip` asset instead of trying to extract it as `tar.gz` ([#9902](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9902) by @risu729).\n- **(docs)** Trim the global settings example in the configuration docs ([#9912](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9912) by @risu729).\n\n## New Contributors\n* @ivov made their first contribution in [#9944](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9944)\n\n## 💚 Sponsor mise\n\nmise is built by [@jdx](https://github.com/jdx) under [**en.dev**](https://en.dev) — an independent studio making developer tooling (mise, [aube](https://aube.en.dev/), and more). Development is funded by sponsors.\n\nIf mise saves you or your team time, please consider sponsoring at [en.dev](https://en.dev). Individual and company sponsorships keep mise fast, free, and independent.\n",

    "tarball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/tarball/v2026.5.10",
    "zipball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/zipball/v2026.5.10",
    "body": "A small release that unblocks s3 backends for users on AWS SSO profiles, plus two minor option-handling fixes that fell out of an internal refactor of the GitHub/GitLab/Forgejo backend.\n\n## Fixed\n\n- **(s3)** s3 backends now work with SSO-based AWS profiles. The `sso` feature of `aws-config` is enabled, so configurations that authenticate via [AWS IAM Identity Center](https://aws.amazon.com/iam/identity-center/) no longer fail with:\n\n  ```\n  S3 error: DispatchFailure { ... ProfileFile provider could not be built:\n  This behavior requires following cargo feature(s) enabled: sso.\n  ```\n\n  ([#9875](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9875) by @Amir-Ahmad).\n- **(backend)** Two small behavior fixes landed while centralizing Git backend option reads ([#9838](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9838) by @risu729):\n  - Forgejo now applies the same install-time option filtering as GitHub/GitLab.\n  - `no_app` is now read through target-aware platform option lookup, so `platforms..no_app = true` is honored when resolving assets for cross-platform lockfiles.\n\n## Changed\n\n- **(backend)** Internal refactor introducing a shared `BackendOptions` reader and a typed option wrapper for the unified GitHub/GitLab/Forgejo backend. No user-visible behavior change beyond the fixes above ([#9838](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9838) by @risu729).\n\n## New Contributors\n\n* @Amir-Ahmad made their first contribution in [#9875](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9875)\n\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/jdx/mise/compare/v2026.5.9...v2026.5.10\n\n## 💚 Sponsor mise\n\nmise is built by [@jdx](https://github.com/jdx) under [**en.dev**](https://en.dev) — an independent studio making developer tooling (mise, [aube](https://aube.en.dev/), and more). Development is funded by sponsors.\n\nIf mise saves you or your team time, please consider sponsoring at [en.dev](https://en.dev). Individual and company sponsorships keep mise fast, free, and independent.\n",

    "tarball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/tarball/v2026.5.9",
    "zipball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/zipball/v2026.5.9",
    "body": "A modest release: SwiftPM gains artifact bundle support, `[[watch_files]]` hooks can pick their own inline shell, and a handful of fixes land for aqua latest-tag resolution, vfox `cmd.exec`, and GitHub OAuth device-flow URLs. Plain-string Tera rendering also gets a fast path.\n\n## Added\n\n- **(spm)** SwiftPM installs now prefer prebuilt artifact bundles (`*.artifactbundle.zip`) when a release publishes one for the current Swift target triple, falling back to a source build otherwise ([#9825](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9825)) by @ikesyo. New controls:\n\n  ```toml\n  [tools]\n  # require an artifact bundle; fail instead of source-building\n  \"spm:giginet/swift-testing-revolutionary\" = { version = \"0.4.0\", artifactbundle = true }\n\n  # always source-build, ignore any bundles\n  \"spm:tuist/tuist\" = { version = \"latest\", artifactbundle = false }\n\n  # disambiguate when multiple bundle assets are published\n  \"spm:org/tool\" = { version = \"1.0.0\", artifactbundle_asset = \"tool.artifactbundle.zip\" }\n\n  [settings]\n  # apply \"bundles only\" globally (mirrors cargo.binstall_only)\n  spm.artifactbundle_only = true\n  ```\n\n- **(config)** `[[watch_files]]` entries with `run` accept an optional `shell` field, rendered through templates and falling back to the configured default inline shell when unset ([#9810](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9810)) by @risu729:\n\n  ```toml\n  [[watch_files]]\n  patterns = [\"*.js\"]\n  run = \"eslint --fix .\"\n  shell = \"bash -c\"\n  ```\n\n  `shell` only applies to `run` hooks; combining it with `task` produces a warning and the value is ignored.\n\n## Fixed\n\n- **(aqua)** When GitHub's `latest` release pointed at a tag that aqua's registry rejected via `version_filter` or `version_constraint`, mise would return it anyway. The latest fast path now applies both checks before accepting a tag ([#9834](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9834)) by @risu729.\n- **(vfox)** Lua `cmd.exec` calls inside vfox plugins now build commands from mise's configured `unix_default_inline_shell_args` / `windows_default_inline_shell_args` instead of hardcoding `sh -c` or `cmd /C`, aligning plugin behavior with tasks, Tera command rendering, and other inline shell users ([#9837](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9837)) by @risu729.\n- GitHub OAuth device-flow paths were slightly off compared to the documented endpoints. The default `oauth_auth_url` is now the GitHub login base, with mise appending `/device/code` and `/oauth/access_token` per [GitHub's device-flow docs](https://docs.github.com/en/apps/oauth-apps/building-oauth-apps/authorizing-oauth-apps#device-flow) ([#9791](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9791)) by @jasisk.\n- **(patrons)** `mise patrons` now points the \"become a patron\" link to the en.dev homepage instead of `/sponsor` ([#9868](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9868)) by @jdx.\n\n## Changed\n\n- **(registry)** `npm` is now resolved through `aqua:npm/cli` (with `npm:npm` retained as a fallback), and `buck2` switches to `aqua:facebook/buck2` with `prerelease = true` so its always-prerelease releases are visible ([#9762](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9762), [#9805](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9805)) by @risu729.\n- **(registry)** Added SonarQube CLI as `aqua:SonarSource/sonarqube-cli` ([#9824](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9824)) by @3PeatVR.\n\n## Performance\n\n- **(config)** Strings with no Tera block markers (`{{`, `{%`, `{#`, including whitespace-trimmed forms) now bypass the Tera renderer at config evaluation sites, skipping context construction, async context fetches, and `get_tera` setup. Tera 1.20.1's grammar guarantees these are the only block openers, so output is unchanged for both well-formed and malformed templates ([#9833](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9833)) by @risu729.\n\n## Documentation\n\n- Updated the Walkthrough guide ([#9853](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9853)) by @thernstig.\n\n## New Contributors\n\n- @3PeatVR made their first contribution in [#9824](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9824)\n- @ikesyo made their first contribution in [#9825](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9825)\n- @thernstig made their first contribution in [#9853](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9853)\n\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/jdx/mise/compare/v2026.5.8...v2026.5.9\n\n## 💚 Sponsor mise\n\nmise is built by [@jdx](https://github.com/jdx) under [**en.dev**](https://en.dev) — an independent studio making developer tooling (mise, [aube](https://aube.en.dev/), and more). Development is funded by sponsors.\n\nIf mise saves you or your team time, please consider sponsoring at [en.dev](https://en.dev). Individual and company sponsorships keep mise fast, free, and independent.\n",

    "tarball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/tarball/v2026.5.8",
    "zipball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/jdx/mise/zipball/v2026.5.8",
    "body": "A small release: a new `mise patrons` command, cleaner task command output when scripts start with a shebang, and a fix for `mise upgrade` summaries getting wiped by progress cleanup. Under the hood, signature verification moves to the modern sigstore-rust stack.\n\n## Added\n\n- **(patrons)** New `mise patrons` subcommand lists individuals on the Patron tier supporting mise development ([#9841](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9841)) by @jdx. Data is fetched from the en.dev patrons feed, cached for 24h, and falls back to stale cache on network failure. Each patron's name renders as a clickable OSC 8 hyperlink in supporting terminals.\n\n  ```\n  $ mise patrons\n  mise is supported by these patrons — thank you\n\n    • Ronald Gierlach\n    • youfoundron\n\n  Become a patron: https://en.dev/sponsor\n  ```\n\n  Flags: `-J/--json`, `--refresh`.\n- **(registry)** Add a `racket` shorthand backed by the aqua `racket/racket/minimal` package, exposing both `racket` and `raco` from the official racket-lang.org release artifacts ([#9784](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9784)) by @albertnetymk.\n\n## Fixed\n\n- **(task)** When a task's `run` body starts with `#!/usr/bin/env bash` or `set -Eeuo pipefail`, the echoed command line would show only that boilerplate and hide the rest of the script. Leading shebang, blank, and `set ...` lines are now skipped when building the displayed command, so the first real command shows up. Execution is unchanged ([#9844](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9844)) by @jdx. Fixes [#9842](https://github.com/jdx/mise/issues/9842).\n\n  ```\n  # before\n  [generate-completions] $ #!/usr/bin/env bash\n\n  # after\n  [generate-completions] $ fzf --fish > ~/.config/fish/completions/fzf.fish\n  ```\n- **(upgrade)** `mise upgrade` could erase its own `Upgraded N tools:` summary detail lines when an upgrade also performed an uninstall — fresh progress jobs registered for the cleanup phase were still active at shutdown, so `stop_clear()` wiped them along with the summary. Progress jobs are now finished and reset before the summary prints ([#9860](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9860)) by @risu729. Regression from [#9779](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9779); addresses [#9856](https://github.com/jdx/mise/discussions/9856).\n\n## Changed\n\n- **(security)** Sigstore verification (`verify_github_attestation`, `verify_cosign_signature`, `verify_slsa_provenance`, `detect_attestations`) now runs on a local `mise-sigstore` adapter built on `sigstore-verify` 0.7 from sigstore-rust, replacing the previous `sigstore-verification` 0.2 dependency ([#9260](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9260)) by @jdx. The mise call sites and helper API are unchanged. The new adapter still covers legacy cosign v1 bundles (e.g. goreleaser-signed releases) and raw DSSE `*.intoto.jsonl` envelopes (slsa-github-generator) that the upstream `Bundle::from_json` rejects.\n\n## Deprecated\n\n- **(config)** The top-level `env_file` setting (and `MISE_ENV_FILE`) is now marked deprecated. Use `env._.file` in `mise.toml` instead ([#9862](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9862)) by @risu729. The JSON Schema gains the `deprecated` keyword, a warning is scheduled for 2026.11.0, and removal is planned for 2027.11.0.\n\n  ```toml\n  # before\n  env_file = \".env\"\n\n  # after\n  [env]\n  _.file = \".env\"\n  ```\n\n## New Contributors\n\n* @albertnetymk made their first contribution in [#9784](https://github.com/jdx/mise/pull/9784)\n\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/jdx/mise/compare/v2026.5.7...v2026.5.8\n\n## 💚 Sponsor mise\n\nmise is built by [@jdx](https://github.com/jdx) under [**en.dev**](https://en.dev) — an independent studio making developer tooling (mise, [aube](https://aube.en.dev/), and more). Development is funded by sponsors.\n\nIf mise saves you or your team time, please consider sponsoring at [en.dev](https://en.dev). Individual and company sponsorships keep mise fast, free, and independent.\n",
